the same way before serving: prefill-2 and decode-0 on g4 could not
unpack the container ("Write on output file failed because No space left
on device" under /data/enroot/data/user-3014). User 3014 is this team
account, ACCOUNT. Rounds 18-20 killed gate steps with timeout several
times, which most likely left unpacked images there; /data is
node-local, cannot be seen from the login node, and compute-node ssh is
not allowed.

Fix in platforms/firmus/framework-comparison/sglang.sbatch: before the
first container step, one plain srun per node (no container) logs
/data, removes this account's pyxis_* directories that do not belong to
the current job, and logs /data again into logs/enroot-cleanup.log. The
two nodes are exclusive to the job, so no running step owns them, and
other accounts' directories are untouched. tests/test_enroot_cleanup.py
runs the exact command against a fake tree (current job and other
accounts kept, earlier jobs removed) and checks that it precedes every
container step.

The gates in 41349758991 also show P3's margin is marginal: adopted on
all 12 GPUs in 28161 (84-cell median 5.48 us), refused in 28164 on one
cell slower than 3 us at 3986+4027. The rule stays as it is.

Everything else is b40711f on the round-22 image c7804efb, without a
build: P revalidated, P3 under the 84-cell rule, the Decode greedy
verify gate with its fixed budget.

CI-style (project CPython 3.13).
